## Scheduled missing keys

## Interpretation

Development cohort: six generated templates, 10 tasks per family and two seeds. No old compute-DL outcomes are pooled. Prompt length is part of the intervention; cold-boot/prompt-cache state does not establish KV-state equality. P arithmetic was omitted: no new P-arithmetic efficacy claim. The observed ≤2 target does not prove population failure ≤1.67%; zero observed P losses does not prove zero population risk. A PASS supports clearer formatting on this limited cohort, not a proven turn-boundary cause, benchmark superiority, or serving-default change.

No post-hoc repair, unwrapping, answer-driven fallback, or grader changes.
